Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the computer_vision space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 802 and the convergence rate is O(878/n^1).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the computer_vision decomposition. By minimality, the element a^802 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 878-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
